        // the offered ones are settings, under [i18n] in config/<env>.toml.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config_with::<crate::plugins::i18n::I18n>("i18n", |config| {
                config
                    .with_paths(true)
                    .keep_unprefixed(crate::modules::admin::MODULE.mount_at)
                    .keep_unprefixed(crate::modules::storage_routes::MODULE.path)
            })
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder.hooks_off_from(
            "i18n",
            "src/modules/i18n.rs",
            crate::modules::i18n::MODULE.hooks.off(),
        );
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::meta::Meta>("meta")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::observability::Observe>("observability")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::db::Database>("db")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::auth::Auth>("auth")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::storage::Files>("storage")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-middleware: CSRF ------------------------------------
        //
        // Not one of the five above. Each of those encodes a fact about
        // this deployment; this one encodes a fact about browsers: any
        // page on any site can post a form here, and the cookies ride
        // along. That is true wherever this runs, so this hook is on, and
        // the only thing to decide is which paths are exempt -- a webhook,
        // or anything else whose caller is not a browser:
        //
        //     exempt = ["/callbacks"]    under [csrf] in config/<env>.toml
        //
        // The token for the request is `ctx.hooked::<CsrfToken>()`;
        // `token.hidden_field()` is the input a form carries.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_middleware::Csrf>("csrf")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::openapi::OpenApi>("openapi")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::validation::Validation>("validation")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::realtime::Realtime>("realtime")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::robots::Robots>("robots")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::sitemap::Sitemap>("sitemap")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-analytics: page views ---------------------------------
        //
        // A request hook, after dispatch: a GET answered with HTML is one
        // page view. It stores no address, no user agent and no cookie; the
        // visitor is a hash whose salt is deleted when the day ends. The
        // views go in two tables of the `[db]` database, written every
        // `flush_interval` seconds, never during a request.
        //
        // The admin is never counted, wherever src/modules/admin.rs
        // mounts it.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config_with::<brocanth_analytics::Analytics>(
                "analytics",
                |config| config.exclude_path(crate::modules::admin::MODULE.mount_at),
            )
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder.hooks_off_from(
            "analytics",
            "src/modules/analytics.rs",
            crate::modules::analytics::MODULE.hooks.off(),
        );
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::model::Model>("model")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::jobs::Jobs>("jobs")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::flags::Flags>("flags")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::auth_oauth::AuthOauth>("auth_oauth")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::superuser::Superuser>("superuser")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- file routes ---------------------------------------------------
        //
        // POST /files stores the `file` part of a form, if `may_upload` in
        // src/plugins/storage_routes/mod.rs says yes. GET /files/{address}
        // serves it to a request with a token the application minted.
        //
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<crate::plugins::storage_routes::StorageRoutes>(
                "storage_routes",
            )
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder.hooks_off_from(
            "storage_routes",
            "src/modules/storage_routes.rs",
            crate::modules::storage_routes::MODULE.hooks.off(),
        );

        // -- brocanth-middleware: security headers ------------------------
        //
        // HSTS (over HTTPS only), nosniff, Referrer-Policy, X-Frame-Options
        // DENY, a Content-Security-Policy and a Permissions-Policy. The
        // policy allows dp-swap's two inline scripts by their SHA-256, so
        // there is no `'unsafe-inline'`. A script you inline yourself needs
        // `.inline_script(TEXT)` on the configuration.
        //
        // HSTS goes out only when the request is known to be HTTPS. Behind
        // a TLS terminator, say so: `behind_tls = true` under
        // [security_headers] in config/<env>.toml.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config_with::<brocanth_middleware::SecurityHeaders>(
                "security_headers",
                |config| {
                    config
                        .inline_script(brocanth_admin::INLINE_SCRIPT)
                        .page_script(brocanth_gtag::SCRIPT, brocanth_gtag::CSP)
                },
            )
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- incoming webhooks ---------------------------------------------
        //
        // Answers POST /webhooks/{source} before the CSRF check: signature,
        // replay window, idempotency key, then the source's handler. The
        // sources are in src/plugins/webhooks_in/mod.rs.
        //
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<crate::plugins::webhooks_in::WebhooksIn>("webhooks_in")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::cache::Cache>("cache")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-audit: change history ---------------------------------
        //
        // A model hook: each change the model layer publishes becomes a row
        // of `brocanth_audit`, written every `flush_secs` seconds, never
        // during a request. Rows older than `retention_days` go once a day.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_audit::AuditPlugin>("audit")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder.hooks_off_from(
            "audit",
            "src/modules/audit.rs",
            crate::modules::audit::MODULE.hooks.off(),
        );
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::mail::MailPlugin>("mail")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::policy::Policies>("policy")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-middleware: rate limit ------------------------------
        //
        // A fixed window per key. Each rule is a group of routes, a limit
        // and a window. THE EXAMPLE RULE IS FOR A LOGIN FORM, not a
        // recommendation: replace it with numbers from your own traffic.
        // The rules are `[[rate_limit.rules]]` in config/<env>.toml.
        //
        // The key is the client address. Behind a proxy, register
        // `ProxyHeaders` above, or name the one header your edge sets and
        // strips in `ip_header`.
        //
        // This counts in this process. With more than one process, use
        // `SharedRateLimit` and `rate_limit::PgStore` (feature `postgres`).
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_middleware::RateLimit>("rate_limit")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder.hooks_off_from(
            "rate_limit",
            "src/modules/middleware_rate_limit.rs",
            crate::modules::middleware_rate_limit::MODULE.hooks.off(),
        );
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::webhooks_out::WebhooksOut>("webhooks_out")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-errors: error pages and reporters --------------------
        //
        // Publishes the reporters another plugin adds to, and reports a
        // hook's failure. A page's failure goes through
        // `crate::plugins::errors::caught`. Whether a debug build shows the
        // cause is a setting, under [errors] in config/<env>.toml.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_errors::Errors>("errors")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder.hooks_off_from(
            "errors",
            "src/modules/errors.rs",
            crate::modules::errors::MODULE.hooks.off(),
        );

        // -- brocanth-middleware: conditional GET -------------------------
        //
        // A weak ETag over the body of every GET that answers 200, and a
        // 304 with no body when the browser sends the same tag back. A
        // page that sets `Last-Modified` also answers If-Modified-Since.
        // The page still renders: this saves the transfer, not the work.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_middleware::ConditionalGet>("conditional_get")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-middleware: flash -----------------------------------
        //
        // Messages for the next page, in a signed cookie. A page writes
        // `ctx.hooked_mut::<brocanth_middleware::Flash>()` and redirects;
        // the next HTML page shows the messages inside the layout's
        // `<div id="flash">`. Other markup: `.render(my_fn)` on the
        // configuration below.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_middleware::FlashPlugin>("flash")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_from_config::<crate::plugins::auth_screens::AuthScreens>("auth_screens")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        // -- brocanth-middleware: compression -----------------------------
        //
        // gzip or brotli, as the browser's Accept-Encoding asks, on a
        // buffered text body of `min_bytes` or more. Never on a stream:
        // an SSE event must not wait for a compressor's window. The media
        // types are an allow-list; the list is `types` under [compression] in config/<env>.toml.
        //
        // Behind a proxy or a CDN that compresses, turn this off: the
        // work is done twice and the second pass finds nothing to gain.
        let builder = builder
            .plugin_hooking_from_config::<brocanth_middleware::Compression>("compression")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        builder
    }};
}
pub(crate) use register;

/// What this binary installs on a request's context, as a type.
///
/// One `Install` per registered plugin, innermost first, which is exactly
/// what `register!` builds. The two have to be the same type: if they
/// drift this file does not compile, which is why the list is written
/// once and both readers derive from it.
///
/// A plugin that puts nothing on the context folds away, because
/// `NoContext::Install<E>` is `E`, so this is `Base` in a project whose
/// plugins all provide nothing.
type WithSessions = After<crate::plugins::sessions::Sessions, Base>;
type WithGtag = After<brocanth_gtag::Gtag, WithSessions>;
type WithAdmin = After<crate::plugins::admin::AdminSettings, WithGtag>;
type WithI18n = After<crate::plugins::i18n::I18n, WithAdmin>;
type WithMeta = After<crate::plugins::meta::Meta, WithI18n>;
type WithObservability = After<crate::plugins::observability::Observe, WithMeta>;
type WithDb = After<crate::plugins::db::Database, WithObservability>;
type WithAuth = After<crate::plugins::auth::Auth, WithDb>;
type WithStorage = After<crate::plugins::storage::Files, WithAuth>;
type WithMiddlewareCsrf = After<brocanth_middleware::Csrf, WithStorage>;
type WithOpenapi = After<crate::plugins::openapi::OpenApi, WithMiddlewareCsrf>;
type WithValidation = After<crate::plugins::validation::Validation, WithOpenapi>;
type WithRealtime = After<crate::plugins::realtime::Realtime, WithValidation>;
type WithRobots = After<crate::plugins::robots::Robots, WithRealtime>;
type WithSitemap = After<crate::plugins::sitemap::Sitemap, WithRobots>;
type WithAnalytics = After<brocanth_analytics::Analytics, WithSitemap>;
type WithModel = After<crate::plugins::model::Model, WithAnalytics>;
type WithJobs = After<crate::plugins::jobs::Jobs, WithModel>;
type WithFlags = After<crate::plugins::flags::Flags, WithJobs>;
type WithAuthOauth = After<crate::plugins::auth_oauth::AuthOauth, WithFlags>;
type WithSuperuser = After<crate::plugins::superuser::Superuser, WithAuthOauth>;
type WithStorageRoutes = After<crate::plugins::storage_routes::StorageRoutes, WithSuperuser>;
type WithMiddlewareSecurityHeaders = After<brocanth_middleware::SecurityHeaders, WithStorageRoutes>;
type WithWebhooksIn = After<crate::plugins::webhooks_in::WebhooksIn, WithMiddlewareSecurityHeaders>;
type WithCache = After<crate::plugins::cache::Cache, WithWebhooksIn>;
type WithAudit = After<brocanth_audit::AuditPlugin, WithCache>;
type WithMail = After<crate::plugins::mail::MailPlugin, WithAudit>;
type WithPolicy = After<crate::plugins::policy::Policies, WithMail>;
type WithMiddlewareRateLimit = After<brocanth_middleware::RateLimit, WithPolicy>;
type WithWebhooksOut = After<crate::plugins::webhooks_out::WebhooksOut, WithMiddlewareRateLimit>;
type WithErrors = After<brocanth_errors::Errors, WithWebhooksOut>;
type WithMiddlewareConditionalGet = After<brocanth_middleware::ConditionalGet, WithErrors>;
type WithFlash = After<brocanth_middleware::FlashPlugin, WithMiddlewareConditionalGet>;
type WithAuthScreens = After<crate::plugins::auth_screens::AuthScreens, WithFlash>;
type WithMiddlewareCompression = After<brocanth_middleware::Compression, WithAuthScreens>;
pub type Installed = WithMiddlewareCompression;

type HookedGtag = Both<<brocanth_gtag::Gtag as HookSource>::Families, NoHooks>;
type HookedI18n = Both<<crate::plugins::i18n::I18n as HookSource>::Families, HookedGtag>;
type HookedCsrf = Both<<brocanth_middleware::Csrf as HookSource>::Families, HookedI18n>;
type HookedAnalytics = Both<<brocanth_analytics::Analytics as HookSource>::Families, HookedCsrf>;
type HookedStorageRoutes =
    Both<<crate::plugins::storage_routes::StorageRoutes as HookSource>::Families, HookedAnalytics>;
type HookedSecurityHeaders =
    Both<<brocanth_middleware::SecurityHeaders as HookSource>::Families, HookedStorageRoutes>;
type HookedWebhooksIn =
    Both<<crate::plugins::webhooks_in::WebhooksIn as HookSource>::Families, HookedSecurityHeaders>;
type HookedAuditPlugin =
    Both<<brocanth_audit::AuditPlugin as HookSource>::Families, HookedWebhooksIn>;
type HookedRateLimit =
    Both<<brocanth_middleware::RateLimit as HookSource>::Families, HookedAuditPlugin>;
type HookedErrors = Both<<brocanth_errors::Errors as HookSource>::Families, HookedRateLimit>;
type HookedConditionalGet =
    Both<<brocanth_middleware::ConditionalGet as HookSource>::Families, HookedErrors>;
type HookedFlashPlugin =
    Both<<brocanth_middleware::FlashPlugin as HookSource>::Families, HookedConditionalGet>;
type HookedCompression =
    Both<<brocanth_middleware::Compression as HookSource>::Families, HookedFlashPlugin>;

/// Which hook families this binary installed, as a type.
///
/// One `Both` per plugin registered with `plugin_hooking`, innermost first,
/// which is exactly the record `register!` builds. A hook family nobody
/// installed costs nothing, and this type is how the compiler knows: every
/// hook entry point opens with a constant `if` on this record.
pub type Hooks = HookedCompression;
/// What this binary puts on the context, said once at startup.
///
/// The lines are a demonstration and they are load-bearing: the installed
/// provider set lives in the application's *type*, so an `extract` for a
/// plugin nobody registered does not compile. That is the modularity claim,
/// checked by the compiler rather than written in a README.
///
/// A handler reads the same values through `Ctx::provided`, which carries
/// the same check. This runs at startup so a misconfiguration is a failure
/// to start rather than a failure on the first request that needed it.
///
/// A macro for the reason `register!` is one: the context's type is the
/// builder's type, and neither is worth spelling.
macro_rules! announce {
    ($app:expr) => {{
        let cx = $app.context();
        let session: &crate::plugins::sessions::Session = cx.extract();
        let cookie = &session.cookie;
        crate::plugins::say!(info, "session cookie: {cookie}"; %cookie);
        let db: &brocanth_db::Db = cx.extract();
        let connections = db.pool().size();
        crate::plugins::say!(info, "db: {connections} connection(s) open"; connections);
        let auth: &crate::plugins::auth::Services = cx.extract();
        let cookie = &auth.sessions().config().cookie_name;
        let providers = auth.providers().len();
        crate::plugins::say!(
            info,
            "auth: session cookie {cookie}, {providers} provider(s)";
            %cookie,
            providers
        );
        let files: &plugins::storage::FileStore = cx.extract();
        let ttl = files.tokens.ttl();
        crate::plugins::say!(info, "storage: ready, download links last {ttl}s"; ttl);
        let checks: &brocanth_schema::Validation = cx.extract();
        let style = checks.report_style();
        crate::plugins::say!(info, "validation: reports as {style:?}"; ?style);
        let realtime: &crate::plugins::realtime::Broadcast = cx.extract();
        let max_clients = realtime.broker.config().max_clients;
        crate::plugins::say!(info, "realtime: up to {max_clients} clients"; max_clients);
        let rules: &crate::plugins::policy::Rules = cx.extract();
        let dialect = &rules.dialect;
        crate::plugins::say!(info, "policy: compiling for {dialect:?}"; ?dialect);
        crate::plugins::warn_hookless($app.pipeline());
    }};
}
pub(crate) use announce;

/// A plugin whose module file turns every hook off: registered, and
/// doing nothing per request. Said once at startup; `brocanth remove`
/// takes the module out.
pub fn warn_hookless(pipeline: &brocanth::plugin_hooks::Pipeline) {
    let all_off = |instance: &str, names: &[&str]| {
        names.iter().all(|name| {
            pipeline
                .skipped()
                .iter()
                .any(|&(i, n)| i == instance && n == *name)
        })
    };
    // Every hook of `analytics` is off in src/modules/analytics.rs.
    if all_off("analytics", &["count", "collect"]) {
        crate::plugins::say!(
            warn,
            "warning: analytics installs no hook; help: brocanth remove analytics"
        );
    }
    // Every hook of `audit` is off in src/modules/audit.rs.
    if all_off("audit", &["record"]) {
        crate::plugins::say!(
            warn,
            "warning: audit installs no hook; help: brocanth remove audit"
        );
    }
    // Every hook of `errors` is off in src/modules/errors.rs.
    if all_off("errors", &["report"]) {
        crate::plugins::say!(
            warn,
            "warning: errors installs no hook; help: brocanth remove errors"
        );
    }
}

/// A line about the application itself: startup, the listener, the stop.
///
/// A `tracing` event, because `observability-logs` is installed: the line is
/// JSON in production and text elsewhere, on stdout, like the request lines.
/// The fields after `;` are the event's fields, in `tracing`'s shorthand
/// (`addr`, `%addr`, `?addr`), and name variables already in scope.
///
/// ```ignore
/// plugins::say!(info, "listening on http://{bound}"; %bound);
/// ```
///
/// Before the observability plugin installs its subscriber (a config file
/// that does not parse, a plugin that fails before it) and with
/// `install_subscriber = false`, an event would go nowhere. Then this is a
/// line on stderr, as it is without `observability-logs`.
macro_rules! say {
    ($level:ident, $message:literal $(; $($field:tt)+)?) => {
        if brocanth_observe::tracing::dispatcher::has_been_set() {
            brocanth_observe::tracing::$level!($($($field)+,)? $message)
        } else {
            eprintln!("{}: {}", crate::app::APP, format_args!($message))
        }
    };
}
pub(crate) use say;

/// One middleware layer, as the stack stores it.
///
/// A name rather than the type written out: clippy calls the bare
/// form a very complex type, and it is also the type `src/app.rs`
/// would have to spell to hold one.
///
/// A layer may wait, so it returns a future. The lifetime is named
/// because the future borrows both the context and the rest of the
/// chain; see `sessions::layer` for the shape of one.
pub type Layer = for<'a> fn(&'a mut Ctx, AsyncNext<'a, Ctx, Response>) -> BoxFuture<'a, Response>;

/// The function behind a middleware name the application mounted.
///
/// Mounting is `src/app.rs`'s decision and stays there: the pattern is a
/// decision and the decision is the application's. Which function answers
/// to `"session"` is not a decision, so it is here, where adding a
/// plugin can write it.
pub fn layer(name: &str) -> Option<Layer> {
    match name {
        "session" => Some(sessions::layer),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// The tabs other modules add to the admin. Each one is behind the module
/// file of the module that adds it: `integrate.admin` in src/modules/.
pub fn admin_panels(mut config: brocanth_admin::AdminConfig) -> brocanth_admin::AdminConfig {
    // "Delete selected" on every model that allows a delete, after a
    // confirmation page. A model leaves it off with
    // `m.without_action("delete_selected")` in src/plugins/admin/mod.rs.
    config = config.action(brocanth_admin::actions::BulkAction::delete_selected());
    // The page views the analytics plugin counts, as a tab.
    if crate::modules::analytics::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = config.panel(brocanth_analytics::admin::AnalyticsPanel::installed());
    }
    // The cache, as a tab: Purge expired, and Clear for each namespace you
    // name with `.namespace(..)`.
    if crate::modules::cache::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = config.panel(brocanth_cache::admin::CachePanel::installed());
    }
    // The queue the jobs plugin runs, as a tab.
    if crate::modules::jobs::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = config.panel(brocanth_jobs::admin::JobsPanel::installed());
    }
    // The feature flags, as a tab that turns each one on or off.
    if crate::modules::flags::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = config.panel(brocanth_flags::admin::FlagsPanel::installed(
            crate::plugins::flags::ALL,
        ));
    }
    // Superusers pass every check; a role holder passes what its roles grant.
    if crate::modules::superuser::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = config.with_policy(brocanth_auth::admin::StaffPolicy);
    }
    // The change history: a tab, a link on every record, every admin write.
    if crate::modules::audit::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = brocanth_audit::admin::install(config);
    }
    // Roles: a superuser defines them and gives them to accounts.
    if crate::modules::roles::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        let roles = brocanth_auth::admin::RolesPanel::late(crate::plugins::superuser::store());
        config = config.panel(roles);
    }
    // The outgoing deliveries, with a Send again button, as a tab.
    if crate::modules::webhooks_out::MODULE.integrate.admin {
        config = config.panel(brocanth_webhooks::admin::DeliveriesPanel::installed());
    }
    config
}

/// One declared setting, by the key it sets.
pub struct Setting {
    /// The key, as `config/<env>.toml` spells it. It is also the flag's name,
    /// unless its module file renames it.
    pub path: &'static str,
    /// The line `--help` prints.
    pub help: &'static str,
    /// Whether a flag may set it. A secret has none: every user on the host
    /// can read a process's arguments.
    pub flag: bool,
    /// The module file that renames its flag and turns the flags on or off.
    pub file: &'static str,
    /// Whether its module reads flags, from that file.
    pub flags: Flags,
    /// A value that means `None` as well as an empty one does.
    pub none_as: Option<&'static str>,
    /// Whether a flag's value stays a string whatever it looks like.
    pub text: bool,
}

/// One value this run was given, above the defaults.
pub struct Given {
    /// What it sets.
    pub setting: &'static Setting,
    /// Where it came from, as typed: `--cookie`, or `$MY_APP_SESSIONS__COOKIE`.
    pub from: String,
    /// The value, as typed. Empty is `None` for an `Option` setting.
    pub value: String,
}

/// The name each flag answers to after the renames in src/modules/, whether
/// the flags are read or not.
///
/// A name that is not spelled as a flag, and two flags under one name, each
/// stop the binary: each would otherwise be a flag that does nothing, or does
/// something else. A rename of a key no flag sets does not compile: `Key` has
/// no variant for it.
fn names() -> Result<Vec<(&'static Setting, &'static str)>, String> {
    let renames = renames();
    let mut names: Vec<(&'static Setting, &'static str)> = Vec::new();
    for setting in SETTINGS.iter().filter(|setting| setting.flag) {
        let name = renames
            .iter()
            .find(|(path, _)| *path == setting.path)
            .map_or(setting.path, |(_, flag)| flag.name());
        if name.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let spelled = name
            .chars()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || "._-".contains(c));
        let taken = ["help", "version", "settings"].contains(&name);
        if !spelled || name.starts_with(['-', '.', '_']) || taken {
            return Err(format!(
                "{} names a flag `{name}`\nhelp: a flag is lower case, digits, `.`, `_` and `-`, starts with a letter or a digit, and is not `help`, `version` or `settings`",
                setting.file
            ));
        }
        if let Some((other, _)) = names.iter().find(|(_, taken)| *taken == name) {
            let files = if other.file == setting.file {
                other.file.to_string()
            } else {
                format!("{} or {}", other.file, setting.file)
            };
            return Err(format!(
                "`--{name}` would set both `{}` and `{}`\nhelp: rename one of them in {files}",
                other.path, setting.path
            ));
        }
        names.push((setting, name));
    }
    Ok(names)
}

/// Every rename in src/modules/, as the setting's path and its flag.
fn renames() -> Vec<(&'static str, Flag)> {
    let mut renames = Vec::new();
    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::brocanth::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));
    }
    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::observability::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));
    }
    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::analytics::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));
    }
    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::sessions::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));
    }
    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::db::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));
    }
    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::jobs::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::flags::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::audit::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::mail::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::auth::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

    for (key, flag) in crate::modules::validation::MODULE.rename {
        renames.push((key.path(), *flag));

/// Whether a module's flags are read: its file says `Flags::On` or
/// `Flags::Off`, or follows the project rule. With a config file, the file is
/// the source until src/modules/brocanth.rs turns the flags on.
fn flags_on(rule: Flags) -> bool {
    match rule {
        Flags::On => true,
        Flags::Off => false,
        Flags::Project => crate::modules::brocanth::MODULE.flags_with_config,
    }
}

/// The flags this run reads: each module file decides, and
/// src/modules/brocanth.rs holds the project rule. Every name is checked
/// first, read or not.
pub fn flag_names() -> Result<Vec<(&'static Setting, &'static str)>, String> {
    Ok(names()?
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(setting, _)| flags_on(setting.flags))
        .collect())
}

/// A flag given while the flags are off, refused by name with what to do
/// instead. Silently ignoring it would run with a value nobody chose.
pub fn refuse_off_flags() -> Result<(), String> {
    let names = names()?;
    for arg in std::env::args_os().skip(1) {
        let arg = arg.to_string_lossy();
        if arg == "--" {
            break;
        }
        let Some(written) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            continue;
        };
        let name = written.split_once('=').map_or(written, |(name, _)| name);
        let Some((setting, _)) = names.iter().find(|(_, known)| *known == name) else {
            continue;
        };
        if flags_on(setting.flags) {
            continue;
        }
        let (section, key) = setting.path.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or(("", setting.path));
        let fix = if setting.file.ends_with("/brocanth.rs") {
            "flags_with_config: true"
        } else {
            "flags: Flags::On"
        };
        return Err(format!(
            "`--{name}` is off: this project reads its settings from config/<env>.toml\nhelp: set `{fix}` in {}, or set `{key}` under [{section}] in config/<env>.toml",
            setting.file
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// The flags this run was given, in the order typed. The environment is not
/// here: `brocanth-config` reads it, under the file and over the flags.
///
/// `--name=VALUE` and `--name VALUE`. Every other argument is left alone: it
/// is a command's, and the command line's own parser has already refused what
/// nobody declared.
pub fn given() -> Result<Vec<Given>, String> {
    let mut given = Vec::new();
    let names = flag_names()?;
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args_os()
        .skip(1)
        .map(|arg| arg.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
        .collect();
    let mut rest = args.iter();
    while let Some(arg) = rest.next() {
        if arg == "--" {
            break;
        }
        let Some(written) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            continue;
        };
        let (name, inline) = match written.split_once('=') {
            Some((name, value)) => (name, Some(value.to_string())),
            None => (written, None),
        };
        let Some((setting, name)) = names.iter().copied().find(|(_, known)| *known == name) else {
            continue;
        };
        let value = match inline {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => rest
                .next()
                .cloned()
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("--{name} needs a value\nhelp: write --{name}=VALUE"))?,
        };
        given.push(Given {
            setting,
            from: format!("--{name}"),
            value,
        });
    }
    Ok(given)
}

/// The configuration this run reads: the files, the environment, and the
/// flags on top where src/modules/ turns them on.
pub fn config() -> Result<brocanth_config::Config, String> {
    refuse_off_flags()?;
    let mut loader = brocanth_config::Loader::new(crate::app::APP);
    for setting in SETTINGS {
        if let Some(alias) = setting.none_as {
            loader = loader.none_as(setting.path, alias);
        }
    }
    for given in given()? {
        let flag = given.from.trim_start_matches('-');
        loader = loader.flag(given.setting.path, flag, &given.value, given.setting.text);
    }
    loader.load().map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

/// The flags, as arguments every command takes.
///
/// A name a command already takes is refused here: `clap` panics on it in a
/// debug build and lets one of the two win in a release one.
pub fn flag_args(
    command: brocanth_command::clap::Command,
) -> brocanth_command::anyhow::Result<brocanth_command::clap::Command> {
    use brocanth_command::{anyhow, clap};

    refuse_off_flags().map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)?;
    let mut command = command;
    for (setting, name) in flag_names().map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)? {
        let taken = command
            .get_subcommands()
            .flat_map(|sub| sub.get_arguments())
            .any(|arg| arg.get_long() == Some(name));
        if taken {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "`--{name}` sets `{}` and is also an argument of a command\nhelp: rename it in {}",
                setting.path,
                setting.file
            );
        }
        command = command.arg(
            clap::Arg::new(name)
                .long(name)
                .value_name("VALUE")
                .help(setting.help)
                .global(true)
                .action(clap::ArgAction::Append),
        );
    }
    Ok(command)
}

/// Every settings layer on its own, lowest first, then the merged values.
/// A secret is masked in every layer.
pub fn settings_report() -> Result<String, String> {
    let config = config()?;
    let mut layers = config.layers().defaults(defaults());
    for setting in SETTINGS.iter().filter(|setting| !setting.flag) {
        layers = layers.secret(setting.path);
    }
    for (path, note) in hooks_off_notes() {
        layers = layers.note(path, note);
    }
    Ok(layers.to_string())
}

/// Each hook src/modules/ turns off, as the setting the settings report
/// marks and the note beside it.
fn hooks_off_notes() -> Vec<(&'static str, String)> {
    let mut notes = Vec::new();
    for name in crate::modules::analytics::MODULE.hooks.off() {
        notes.push((
            "analytics.enabled",
            format!("hook analytics.{name} is off in src/modules/analytics.rs"),
        ));
    }
    for name in crate::modules::audit::MODULE.hooks.off() {
        notes.push((
            "audit.retention_days",
            format!("hook audit.{name} is off in src/modules/audit.rs"),
        ));
    }
    for name in crate::modules::storage_routes::MODULE.hooks.off() {
        notes.push((
            "storage_routes.max_bytes",
            format!("hook storage_routes.{name} is off in src/modules/storage_routes.rs"),
        ));
    }
    for name in crate::modules::middleware_rate_limit::MODULE.hooks.off() {
        notes.push((
            "rate_limit.ip_header",
            format!("hook rate_limit.{name} is off in src/modules/middleware_rate_limit.rs"),
        ));
    }
    for name in crate::modules::i18n::MODULE.hooks.off() {
        notes.push((
            "i18n.query",
            format!("hook i18n.{name} is off in src/modules/i18n.rs"),
        ));
    }
    for name in crate::modules::errors::MODULE.hooks.off() {
        notes.push((
            "errors.details",
            format!("hook errors.{name} is off in src/modules/errors.rs"),
        ));
    }
    notes
}

/// `[server]`: the settings the framework reads itself. It declares them as
/// a module declares its own, so each has a flag, a variable and a line in
/// the settings report.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ServerSettings {
    /// Seconds a stop waits for open requests, then for the plugins.
    pub shutdown_grace_secs: u64,
}

impl Default for ServerSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            shutdown_grace_secs: crate::app::SHUTDOWN_GRACE.as_secs(),
        }
    }
}

/// `[server] shutdown_grace_secs` from the files, the environment and the
/// flags, or [`SHUTDOWN_GRACE`](crate::app::SHUTDOWN_GRACE) when none sets it.
pub fn shutdown_grace() -> Result<std::time::Duration, String> {
    let config = config()?;
    let Some(value) = config
        .raw_section("server")
        .and_then(|server| server.get("shutdown_grace_secs"))
    else {
        return Ok(crate::app::SHUTDOWN_GRACE);
    };
    // An integer in a file, a flag or a variable; a string when a layer
    // could not type it.
    value
        .as_integer()
        .and_then(|secs| u64::try_from(secs).ok())
        .or_else(|| value.as_str().and_then(|secs| secs.trim().parse().ok()))
        .map(std::time::Duration::from_secs)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            format!(
                "`[server] shutdown_grace_secs` is {}, and it must be a whole number of seconds\nhelp: set it to a whole number of seconds, such as 25",
                value.display()
            )
        })
}

/// What the server is handed for one request.
pub type Answer = brocanth::plugin_routes::BoxFuture<'static, brocanth_server::Response>;

/// A handler, as one server wrap hands it to the next.
pub type Handler = std::sync::Arc<dyn Fn(brocanth_server::Request) -> Answer + Send + Sync>;

/// Something around the whole server: it sees every request, a static
/// file included, outside the request hooks.
///
/// A module writes a type that implements it and names the type in
/// `Slot::ServerWrap`. Only `wrap` is required.
pub trait ServerWrap {
    /// `next`, with this wrap around it.
    fn wrap(app: &std::sync::Arc<crate::app::App>, next: Handler) -> Handler;

    /// Listeners of its own, beside the main one. Called once, after the
    /// plugins started.
    fn side_listeners(
        app: &crate::app::App,
    ) -> impl std::future::Future<Output = Result<(), String>> {
        let _ = app;
        async { Ok(()) }
    }

    /// Called when a stop begins, before the listener closes. A wrap that
    /// must keep answering for a while -- a readiness probe that fails
    /// first -- waits here.
    fn before_stop(app: &crate::app::App) -> impl std::future::Future<Output = ()> {
        let _ = app;
        async {}
    }
}

/// What `serve` answers a request with: `next`, inside each server wrap.
/// The first is the outermost: it sees a request first and the response
/// last.
///
/// - `observability::serving::Serving`
/// - `static_files::StaticFiles`
pub fn wrap<F, Fut>(
    app: &std::sync::Arc<crate::app::App>,
    next: F,
) -> impl Fn(brocanth_server::Request) -> Answer + Send + Sync + 'static
where
    F: Fn(brocanth_server::Request) -> Fut + Send + Sync + 'static,
    Fut: std::future::Future<Output = brocanth_server::Response> + Send + 'static,
{
    let next: Handler = std::sync::Arc::new(move |request| -> Answer { Box::pin(next(request)) });
    let next = <static_files::StaticFiles as ServerWrap>::wrap(app, next);
    let next = <observability::serving::Serving as ServerWrap>::wrap(app, next);
    move |request| next(request)
}

/// Each wrap's own listeners, in order.
pub async fn side_listeners(app: &crate::app::App) -> Result<(), String> {
    <observability::serving::Serving as ServerWrap>::side_listeners(app).await?;
    <static_files::StaticFiles as ServerWrap>::side_listeners(app).await?;
    Ok(())
}

/// The first half of a stop: each wrap's `before_stop`, in order, then the
/// listener closes.
pub async fn stop_accepting(app: &crate::app::App, stopper: brocanth_server::Stopper) {
    <observability::serving::Serving as ServerWrap>::before_stop(app).await;
    <static_files::StaticFiles as ServerWrap>::before_stop(app).await;
    stopper.stop();
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod flag_tests {
    /// A rename in src/modules/ that is not spelled as a flag, or two flags
    /// under one name, stops the binary at startup. This finds it first.
    #[test]
    fn flag_names_are_valid() {
        if let Err(message) = super::names() {
            panic!("{message}");
        }
    }
}

// brocanth-digest: 8079a1b70a8d55b6
===== src/plugins/shape.rs =====
//! Written by `brocanth`. Do not edit -- `brocanth add` and `brocanth remove` rewrite this file.
//! Your choices go in src/modules/<module>.rs; this file is their shape.
//!
//! The shape of each file in src/modules/: one Rust module per file.
//!
//! Each type has a `DEFAULT`, and each file ends with `..Module::DEFAULT`, so
//! a field that a later version adds never breaks a file that predates it.

// A choice this project does not read, such as an integration with a module
// it does not have, is not dead code. `Flag` and `Flags` are in each module
// for its file's one `use` line.
#![allow(dead_code, unused_imports)]

/// Whether a module's settings have flags.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Flags {
    /// The project rule: on without a config file; with one,
    /// `flags_with_config` in src/modules/brocanth.rs.
    Project,
    /// On, beside a config file too.
    On,
    /// Off. The variable and the config file still set each value.
    Off,
}

/// The flag of one setting, when it is not the declared name.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Flag {
    /// `--<name>` in place of `--<section>.<key>`.
    As(&'static str),
    /// No flag. The variable and the config key still set the value.
    Hidden,
}

impl Flag {
    /// The name the flag answers to. Empty for no flag.
    pub const fn name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Flag::As(name) => name,
            Flag::Hidden => "",
        }
    }
}

/// The shape of src/modules/brocanth.rs.
pub mod brocanth {
    pub use super::{Flag, Flags};

    /// A setting of `[server]` that has a flag. A secret or a list has no flag,
    /// so it has no variant here and cannot be renamed.
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    pub enum Key {
        ShutdownGraceSecs,
    }

    impl Key {
        /// The setting, as `config/<env>.toml` and the flag spell it.
        pub const fn path(self) -> &'static str {
            match self {
                Key::ShutdownGraceSecs => "server.shutdown_grace_secs",
            }
        }
    }

    /// What src/modules/brocanth.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {
        /// With the `config` module, whether `Flags::Project` reads flags.
        pub flags_with_config: bool,
        /// Flags under another name, or no flag.
        pub rename: &'static [(Key, Flag)],
    }

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            flags_with_config: false,
            rename: &[],
        };
    }
}

/// The shape of src/modules/analytics.rs.
pub mod analytics {
    pub use super::{Flag, Flags};

    /// A setting of `[analytics]` that has a flag. A secret or a list has no
    /// flag, so it has no variant here and cannot be renamed.
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    pub enum Key {
        Enabled,
        Site,
        RetentionDays,
        FlushInterval,
        RespectGpc,
        IpHeader,
        CountryHeader,
        Migrate,
        CollectorPath,
    }

    impl Key {
        /// The setting, as `config/<env>.toml` and the flag spell it.
        pub const fn path(self) -> &'static str {
            match self {
                Key::Enabled => "analytics.enabled",
                Key::Site => "analytics.site",
                Key::RetentionDays => "analytics.retention_days",
                Key::FlushInterval => "analytics.flush_interval",
                Key::RespectGpc => "analytics.respect_gpc",
                Key::IpHeader => "analytics.ip_header",
                Key::CountryHeader => "analytics.country_header",
                Key::Migrate => "analytics.migrate",
                Key::CollectorPath => "analytics.collector_path",
            }
        }
    }

    /// The hooks of `analytics` that a file may turn off. A hook the module
    /// cannot work without has no field.
    pub struct Hooks {
        /// `analytics.count`: count each page this server answers.
        pub count: bool,
        /// `analytics.collect`: accept beacons at `collector_path` from
        /// `collector_origins`.
        pub collect: bool,
    }

    impl Hooks {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            count: true,
            collect: true,
        };

        /// The hook names this file turns off, for `AppBuilder::hooks_off`.
        pub fn off(&self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
            let mut off = Vec::new();
            if !self.count {
                off.push("count");
            }
            if !self.collect {
                off.push("collect");
            }
            off
        }
    }

    /// The code `analytics` writes into another module. A field exists whether
    /// that module is in the project or not, and does nothing without it.
    pub struct Integrate {
        /// With `admin`: a tab at /admin/analytics with the page-view numbers.
        pub admin: bool,
    }

    impl Integrate {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self { admin: true };
    }

    /// What src/modules/analytics.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {
        /// Whether the settings of this module have flags.
        pub flags: Flags,
        /// Flags under another name, or no flag.
        pub rename: &'static [(Key, Flag)],
        /// The hooks that run.
        pub hooks: Hooks,
        /// The code written into another module.
        pub integrate: Integrate,
    }

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            flags: Flags::Project,
            rename: &[],
            hooks: Hooks::DEFAULT,
            integrate: Integrate::DEFAULT,
        };
    }
}

/// The shape of src/modules/audit.rs.
pub mod audit {
    pub use super::{Flag, Flags};

    /// A setting of `[audit]` that has a flag. A secret or a list has no flag,
    /// so it has no variant here and cannot be renamed.
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    pub enum Key {
        RetentionDays,
        FlushSecs,
        Migrate,
    }

    impl Key {
        /// The setting, as `config/<env>.toml` and the flag spell it.
        pub const fn path(self) -> &'static str {
            match self {
                Key::RetentionDays => "audit.retention_days",
                Key::FlushSecs => "audit.flush_secs",
                Key::Migrate => "audit.migrate",
            }
        }
    }

    /// The hooks of `audit` that a file may turn off. A hook the module
    /// cannot work without has no field.
    pub struct Hooks {
        /// `audit.record`: record each change the model layer publishes through
        /// `HookSink`.
        pub record: bool,
    }

    impl Hooks {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self { record: true };

        /// The hook names this file turns off, for `AppBuilder::hooks_off`.
        pub fn off(&self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
            let mut off = Vec::new();
            if !self.record {
                off.push("record");
            }
            off
        }
    }

    /// The code `audit` writes into another module. A field exists whether
    /// that module is in the project or not, and does nothing without it.
    pub struct Integrate {
        /// With `admin`: a History tab at /admin/history, a History link on every
        /// record, and every admin write recorded with its values.
        pub admin: bool,
    }

    impl Integrate {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self { admin: true };
    }

    /// What src/modules/audit.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {
        /// Whether the settings of this module have flags.
        pub flags: Flags,
        /// Flags under another name, or no flag.
        pub rename: &'static [(Key, Flag)],
        /// The hooks that run.
        pub hooks: Hooks,
        /// The code written into another module.
        pub integrate: Integrate,
    }

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            flags: Flags::Project,
            rename: &[],
            hooks: Hooks::DEFAULT,
            integrate: Integrate::DEFAULT,
        };
    }
}

            max_failures: 10,

    }

/// The shape of src/modules/storage_routes.rs.
pub mod storage_routes {
    pub use super::{Flag, Flags};

    /// A setting of `[storage_routes]` that has a flag. A secret or a list has
    /// no flag, so it has no variant here and cannot be renamed.
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    pub enum Key {
        MaxBytes,
        CsrfField,
        CsrfHeader,
    }

    impl Key {
        /// The setting, as `config/<env>.toml` and the flag spell it.
        pub const fn path(self) -> &'static str {
            match self {
                Key::MaxBytes => "storage_routes.max_bytes",
                Key::CsrfField => "storage_routes.csrf_field",
                Key::CsrfHeader => "storage_routes.csrf_header",
            }
        }
    }

    /// The hooks of `storage-routes` that a file may turn off. A hook the module
    /// cannot work without has no field.
    pub struct Hooks {
        /// `storage_routes.lift`: copy an upload form's CSRF field into the header
        /// the CSRF check reads.
        pub lift: bool,
    }

    impl Hooks {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self { lift: true };

        /// The hook names this file turns off, for `AppBuilder::hooks_off`.
        pub fn off(&self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
            let mut off = Vec::new();
            if !self.lift {
                off.push("lift");
            }
            off
        }
    }

    /// What src/modules/storage_routes.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {
        /// Whether the settings of this module have flags.
        pub flags: Flags,
        /// Flags under another name, or no flag.
        pub rename: &'static [(Key, Flag)],
        /// The hooks that run.
        pub hooks: Hooks,
        /// Where the routes answer: uploads at {path}, files at {path}/{address}.
        pub path: &'static str,
        /// Serve a file to a request with no token. Off, every download needs a
        /// token.
        pub public: bool,
        /// Answer ?thumb=WxH with a thumbnail, rendered once per size.
        pub thumbnails: bool,
    }

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            flags: Flags::Project,
            rename: &[],
            hooks: Hooks::DEFAULT,
            path: "/files",
            public: false,
            thumbnails: true,
        };
    }
}

/// The shape of src/modules/i18n.rs.
pub mod i18n {
    pub use super::{Flag, Flags};

    /// A setting of `[i18n]` that has a flag. A secret or a list has no flag,
    /// so it has no variant here and cannot be renamed.
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    pub enum Key {
        Query,
        Cookie,
        Default,
        Remember,
    }

    impl Key {
        /// The setting, as `config/<env>.toml` and the flag spell it.
        pub const fn path(self) -> &'static str {
            match self {
                Key::Query => "i18n.query",
                Key::Cookie => "i18n.cookie",
                Key::Default => "i18n.default",
                Key::Remember => "i18n.remember",
            }
        }
    }

    /// The hooks of `i18n` that a file may turn off. A hook the module
    /// cannot work without has no field.
    pub struct Hooks {
        /// `i18n.content-language`: send Content-Language and Vary, and keep a
        /// locale the query chose in the cookie.
        pub content_language: bool,
    }

    impl Hooks {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            content_language: true,
        };

        /// The hook names this file turns off, for `AppBuilder::hooks_off`.
        pub fn off(&self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
            let mut off = Vec::new();
            if !self.content_language {
                off.push("content-language");
            }
            off
        }
    }

    /// What src/modules/i18n.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {
        /// Whether the settings of this module have flags.
        pub flags: Flags,
        /// Flags under another name, or no flag.
        pub rename: &'static [(Key, Flag)],
        /// The hooks that run.
        pub hooks: Hooks,
    }

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            flags: Flags::Project,
            rename: &[],
            hooks: Hooks::DEFAULT,
        };
    }
}

/// The shape of src/modules/i18n_routing.rs.
pub mod i18n_routing {
    /// What src/modules/i18n_routing.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {}

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {};
    }

    }

    /// The hooks of `errors` that a file may turn off. A hook the module
    /// cannot work without has no field.
    pub struct Hooks {
        /// `errors.report`: tell the reporters about a hook's failure.
        pub report: bool,
    }

    impl Hooks {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self { report: true };

        /// The hook names this file turns off, for `AppBuilder::hooks_off`.
        pub fn off(&self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
            let mut off = Vec::new();
            if !self.report {
                off.push("report");
            }
            off
        }
    }

    /// What src/modules/errors.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {
        /// Whether the settings of this module have flags.
        pub flags: Flags,
        /// Flags under another name, or no flag.
        pub rename: &'static [(Key, Flag)],
        /// The hooks that run.
        pub hooks: Hooks,
    }

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {
            flags: Flags::Project,
            rename: &[],
            hooks: Hooks::DEFAULT,
        };
    }
}

/// The shape of src/modules/docker.rs.
pub mod docker {
    /// What src/modules/docker.rs holds.
    pub struct Module {}

    impl Module {
        /// The value of each field the file does not name.
        pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self {};
    }
}

===== src/modules/brocanth.rs =====
//! The framework itself: the flag rule of the project, and `[server]`.
//!
//! Written once by `brocanth`. This file is yours: no command rewrites it.
//! A change here takes effect on the next `cargo build`.

// `Flag` and `Key` are for `rename`: unused until it names a setting.
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use crate::plugins::shape::brocanth::{Flag, Key, Module};

// Every field is written out so you can read the choices. The last line
// gives a default to a field that a later version of the module adds.
#[allow(clippy::needless_update)]
pub const MODULE: Module = Module {
    // With the `config` module, whether the binary reads flags too. Off:
    // the file and the environment are the sources, and a flag is refused
    // with a pointer to this line. Without `config`, flags are always read.
    // A module file with `Flags::On` or `Flags::Off` overrides this.
    flags_with_config: false,

    // Another name for a flag, or no flag. The variable and the config key
    // keep the declared name. Examples:
    //   (Key::ShutdownGraceSecs, Flag::As("shutdown-grace-secs")),
    //   (Key::ShutdownGraceSecs, Flag::Hidden),
    rename: &[],

    ..Module::DEFAULT
};

    //   (Key::BaseUrl, Flag::Hidden),
    rename: &[],

    // Where the sign-in routes answer. The redirect URI at each provider is
    // <base_url><mount_at>/<provider>/callback.
    mount_at: "/oauth",

    ..Module::DEFAULT
};

        // `storage_routes.lift`: copy an upload form's CSRF field into the
        // header the CSRF check reads.
        lift: true,
        ..Hooks::DEFAULT
    },

    // Where the routes answer: uploads at {path}, files at {path}/{address}.
    path: "/files",

    // Serve a file to a request with no token. Off, every download needs a
    // token.
    public: false,

    // Answer ?thumb=WxH with a thumbnail, rendered once per size.
    thumbnails: true,

    ..Module::DEFAULT
};

===== config/development.toml =====
# The configuration of this environment.
#
# The framework owns `[app]` and `[server]`. Every other section is claimed by
# the plugin instance registered under that name in src/main.rs, and a section
# nothing claims stops the build rather than being silently ignored -- a
# misspelt section must never build clean.
#
# Layered, highest precedence first: $MY_BLOG_CONFIG, /etc/my_blog/development.toml,
# ./my_blog_development.toml, ./config/development.toml. $MY_BLOG_ENV picks the
# environment; without it, development.

[app]
name = "My Blog"

[server]
# Seconds a stop (SIGTERM or Ctrl-C) waits for open requests, then for the
# plugins. Keep it below the orchestrator's kill timeout: Kubernetes waits 30.
shutdown_grace_secs = 25

[sessions]
# The session cookie's name.
cookie = "_my_blog_session"
# Send the cookie over HTTPS only.
# Development only. Anywhere a browser reaches over HTTPS, this is true.
secure = false

[gtag]
# Insert the consent banner and the Google tag into every HTML page.
# Off outside production, so a developer's visits are not counted.
enabled = false
# The GA4 measurement id, `G-` then capitals and digits.
measurement_id = ""
# `basic`: no Google script before consent. `advanced`: cookieless pings before it.
consent_mode = "basic"
# Treat Global Privacy Control and Do Not Track as a refusal.
respect_gpc = true
# How long a choice is kept before the banner asks again, in days.
# Six months is what the CNIL recommends.
consent_days = 182
# Send the page title. Off: the scrubbed path stands in for it.
send_title = false
# The query parameters that reach Google; every other one is removed.
allow_params = ["utm_source", "utm_medium", "utm_campaign", "utm_term", "utm_content"]
# Paths that never get the tag, each with everything under it: "/drafts".
# The admin never gets the tag: src/plugins/mod.rs reads its path from
# src/modules/admin.rs.
exclude = []
# The domain Google's cookies live on, such as `example.co.uk`; none lets the tag pick it. Empty means none.
# cookie_domain = ""
# A file each consent choice is appended to, such as `log/consent.jsonl`. Empty means none.
# A server only: a static export has no one to report a choice to.
# consent_log = ""

[gtag.banner]
# The sentence the banner asks with.
text = "We would like to measure visits with Google Analytics. It sets cookies only if you accept."
# The button that accepts.
accept = "Accept"
# The button that refuses.
refuse = "Refuse"
# The button that opens the banner again.
manage = "Cookie settings"
# A link to the privacy policy, such as `/privacy`. Empty means none.
# policy_url = ""
# The text of that link.
policy = "Privacy policy"

[admin]
# The end of every <title>, and the header and index heading unless set.
title = "My Blog admin"
# What the top of the sidebar says. Empty means none.
# header = "Acme backoffice"
# The index page's heading. Empty means none.
# index_title = "Site administration"
# Where the sidebar's "View site" link points. Empty means none.
# site_url = "/"
# A stylesheet every screen links after the shipped one: the theme. Empty means none.
# stylesheet = "/admin-theme.css"

[i18n]
# The query parameter that names a locale: `?locale=fr`. Empty means none.
# query = "locale"
# The cookie that names a locale. Empty means none.
# cookie = "locale"
# The locale for a request that asks for none offered. None: `en`, the catalog's default. Empty means none.
# A static export sends no Accept-Language, so this is the language of every exported page.
# default = ""
# The locales a request may get, by tag. Empty: every file in locales/.
# A catalog left out still compiles, so a translation in progress ships unseen.
available = []
# A `?locale=` choice sets the cookie, so the next page keeps it.
remember = true
# Path prefixes that keep no locale segment and are never redirected.
# A path whose last segment has a `.` (/robots.txt) is never redirected either,
# nor a socket or a stream. The routes of other modules are kept in
# src/plugins/mod.rs, from their files in src/modules/.
unprefixed = ["/api"]

[meta]
# The site's name: the title of a page with none, and og:site_name.
name = "My Blog"
# How a page's title becomes the document's. `%s` is the page's own title. Empty means none.
# title_template = "%s | My Blog"
# The site's address, scheme and host. A canonical URL and og:url need it. Empty means none.
# Unset, a page has no canonical URL: a relative one is worse than none.
# base_url = "https://example.com"
# The description of a page that gives none. Empty means none.
# description = ""
# The og:image of a page that gives none: a path or a full URL. Empty means none.
# image = "/og.png"
# The site's account, for twitter:site. Empty means none.
# twitter = "@example"

[observability]
# json or text. Empty: json in production, text elsewhere.
# format = "json"
# A level, or per-target levels: info,sqlx=warn. RUST_LOG wins when set.
level = "info"
# Liveness. Empty turns it off.
live_path = "/up"
# Readiness: every plugin's check. Empty turns it off.
ready_path = "/readyz"
# Seconds to answer "not ready" after SIGTERM, before the listener stops.
drain_delay_secs = 0
# Prometheus text. Empty turns it off.
metrics_path = "/metrics"
# When set, /metrics needs `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.
# metrics_token = "a long random string"
# When set, /metrics is served on this address, not the main listener.
# metrics_bind = "127.0.0.1:9100"
# The collector. Empty: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT, then localhost:4318.
# otlp_endpoint = "http://localhost:4318"
# The share of new traces kept, from 0.0 to 1.0.
sample_ratio = 1.0

[db]
# The Postgres URL: postgres://user:password@host:5432/database.
# A URL can carry a password, so no flag sets it.
url = "postgres://localhost/development"
# The most connections the pool opens.
# A statement holds a connection only while it runs, so this bounds
# concurrent statements, not concurrent requests.
max_connections = 10
# The connections the pool keeps open when idle.
min_connections = 0
# Seconds a statement waits for a connection, opening one included.
acquire_timeout = 30
# Seconds before an idle connection closes. 0 keeps it open.
idle_timeout = 600
# Seconds before the server cancels a statement. 0 is no limit.
statement_timeout = 0
# The schemas for a table name with no schema. Empty is the server's default.
search_path = []
# Every transaction is read-only: for a replica, or a reporting process.
read_only = false
# The name pg_stat_activity shows. Empty sends none.
application_name = "my-blog"
# A PEM bundle to trust, with verify-full. Empty means none.
# Unset, the pool trusts the system's roots.
# ca_file = "/etc/ssl/certs/db-ca.pem"

[auth]
# The Postgres database for accounts and sessions.
# The tables are created at startup, named brocanth_auth_*. A URL can
# carry a password, so no flag sets it.
url = "postgres://localhost/development"
# The name of the variable that holds the session signing key.
# Not the key. 32 bytes, base64url. `brocanth` will not invent one: a key
# minted at startup logs every session out on each restart and differs
# between the instances of a fleet, so a missing key stops the application.
#
#   export MY_BLOG_SESSION_KEY=$(head -c 32 /dev/urandom | base64 | tr '+/' '-_' | tr -d '=')
signing_key_env = "MY_BLOG_SESSION_KEY"
# The session cookie's name.
# Development, over plain HTTP on localhost. In production this is
# "__Host-session" with secure = true, and the two move together: a
# __Host- cookie that is not Secure is a cookie no browser stores.
cookie_name = "session"
# Send the cookie over HTTPS only.
secure = false
# Seconds of inactivity before a session is dead.
# Eight hours unattended, thirty days total. Idle bounds a browser nobody is
# sitting at; absolute bounds a stolen cookie that is being kept warm.
idle_timeout = 28800
# Seconds from creation before a session is dead, however much it was used.
absolute_timeout = 2592000

[storage]
# Where uploads are stored.
# Below the working directory, so an upload is not source and `.gitignore`
# already says so. Move it off the deployment's disk and every instance stops
# seeing the same files: an object store is a separate crate behind the same
# trait, and swapping to it is a change in src/plugins/storage/mod.rs alone.
root = "var/uploads"
# Signs file tokens and keys object addresses.
# Development only, and the one line to change first. While it is this
# string, anybody who has read this repository can mint a token for any
# object, and anybody holding a copy of a file can compute whether somebody
# uploaded it. No flag sets it: a flag is readable by every user on the host.
secret = "development only: replace before anything is uploaded in anger"
# Seconds a file token lives.
# It cannot be revoked, so this number is the window in which a URL pasted
# into a chat still works. Two minutes is long enough for a page full of
# images and short enough to be dead before anyone clicks it.
token_ttl = 120

[csrf]
# The cookie this hook owns.
cookie = "csrf_token"
# The form field the token comes back in.
field = "_csrf_token"
# The header a fetch caller may send instead of the field. Empty means none.
# While it is set, the cookie is not HttpOnly: a script reads it.
# header = "x-csrf-token"
# Paths never checked, each with everything under it.
# For a caller that is not a browser: a webhook, a device.
exempt = []
# Send the cookie over HTTPS only.
secure = true

[openapi]
# The name of the API.
# Not the name of this binary: the document is read by somebody who never
# sees the source.
title = "My Blog"
# The version of the contract.
# Not of the crate. It moves when an operation changes and stays still
# when the code does.
version = "0.1.0"

[validation]
# How a failed check is reported: json or form.
# json for an API client, form for re-rendering a form with the
# messages grouped by field. A third spelling stops the
# application at startup rather than reporting the wrong way.
report = "json"

[realtime]
# How many connections the broker holds.
# Refused at the limit, not traded for an existing connection: evicting a
# subscriber to admit a new one lets anyone holding a socket knock everybody
# else off. The memory is max_clients * queue * the size of a frame.
max_clients = 1000
# How many topics one client may hold.
# A page asking for more than this is almost certainly asking row by row for
# something the collection wildcard covers.
max_subscriptions_per_client = 64
# How many frames a mailbox holds before the overflow policy fires.
queue = 32
# What a full mailbox does: drop-oldest, drop-newest or disconnect.
# The reader is told how many frames it missed either way, so a stale view
# is a thing the client can see.
overflow = "drop-oldest"

[robots]
# Let crawlers read this site. False answers `Disallow: /` and nothing else.

# the plugin's default, true, unless its own file says otherwise. Set it
# false on staging, or a search engine lists staging beside production.
index = false
# Paths no crawler may read, each from the root: "/admin".
disallow = []
# Paths a crawler may read under a disallowed one: "/admin/help".
allow = []
# Where the sitemap is, as a full URL. Empty means none.
# sitemap = "https://example.com/sitemap.xml"

# More groups, for named crawlers, written after the `*` group. One table
# per group. A crawler that matches a named group follows that group only,
# not the `*` rules. robots.txt is a request: a crawler may ignore it.
# Example: ask crawlers that collect training data to read nothing.
# [[robots.groups]]
# agents = ["GPTBot", "CCBot", "Google-Extended", "ClaudeBot"]
# disallow = ["/"]

===== src/commands/serve.rs =====
//! `serve` -- the one command the scaffold wrote.
//!
//! It is ordinary project code: rename it, give it more flags, or delete it.
//! The framework does not own this command line.

use brocanth_command::{anyhow, clap, cmd, Command, Context};

/// Serve the application over HTTP.
#[cmd]
#[derive(clap::Args)]
pub struct Serve {
    /// Address to bind.
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = crate::app::default_addr())]
    pub addr: String,
}

impl Command for Serve {
    fn run(self, _ctx: &mut Context) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        crate::app::serve(&self.addr).map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)
    }
}
===== src/commands/settings.rs =====
//! `settings` -- every settings layer on its own, then the merged values.
//!
//! Ordinary project code, like `serve`: rename it or delete it, and delete
//! its line in `src/commands/mod.rs`.

use brocanth_command::{anyhow, clap, cmd, Command, Context};

/// Show each settings layer, lowest precedence first, then the merged values.
#[cmd]
#[derive(clap::Args)]
pub struct Settings {}

impl Command for Settings {
    fn run(self, _ctx: &mut Context) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        print!(
            "{}",
            crate::plugins::settings_report().map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)?
        );
        Ok(())
    }
}

===== .github/workflows/ci.yml =====
# Every push and every pull request: formatting, lints, tests.
#
# The toolchain is the one `rust-toolchain.toml` pins. A dependency on a path
# outside this repository (`brocanth create --brocanth-path`) is not in the
# checkout, and the build fails; depend on git instead.
name: ci

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

env:
  CARGO_TERM_COLOR: always

jobs:
  check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:16
        env:
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
        ports: ["5432:5432"]
        options: >-
          --health-cmd pg_isready --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
    env:
      # The tests that need Postgres read this; without it they skip.
      BROCANTH_TEST_DATABASE_URL: postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/postgres
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      # Reads rust-toolchain.toml, and caches the target directory.
      - uses: actions-rust-lang/setup-rust-toolchain@v1
        with:
          components: rustfmt, clippy
      - run: cargo fmt --all --check
      - run: cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings
      - run: cargo test

  image:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3
      # Builds the image and pushes nothing. Add a registry login and
      # `push: true` when there is somewhere to push to.
      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          push: false
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
===== Dockerfile =====
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1
#
# The image of My Blog.
#
#   docker build -t my-blog .
#   docker run --rm -p 3000:3000 my-blog
#
# Three stages. `planner` reduces the manifests to a recipe. `builder` builds
# the dependencies from the recipe, in a layer that stays cached until
# Cargo.toml or Cargo.lock changes, and then builds the application. The
# runtime holds the binary, the config directory, the migrations directory
# (`migrate up` reads SQL migrations from it) and nothing else.
#
# Dependencies on a path outside this directory (`brocanth create
# --brocanth-path`) are not in the build context, and the build fails. Build
# from git dependencies, or add the checkout to the context.

ARG RUST_VERSION=1.98

FROM rust:${RUST_VERSION}-slim-bookworm AS chef
RUN cargo install cargo-chef --locked
WORKDIR /app

FROM chef AS planner
COPY . .
RUN cargo chef prepare --recipe-path recipe.json

FROM chef AS builder
COPY --from=planner /app/recipe.json recipe.json
RUN cargo chef cook --release --recipe-path recipe.json
COPY . .
RUN cargo build --release --bin my-blog \
 && mkdir -p config migrations

FROM debian:bookworm-slim AS runtime
# ca-certificates for TLS to a database or an API; curl for HEALTHCHECK.
RUN apt-get update \
 && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends ca-certificates curl \
 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
RUN useradd --system --uid 10001 --home-dir /app --shell /usr/sbin/nologin app
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=builder /app/target/release/my-blog /app/my-blog
COPY --from=builder /app/config /app/config
COPY --from=builder /app/migrations /app/migrations
USER 10001

# Every interface, inside the container. `$MY_BLOG_ENV` picks
# config/production.toml, and a variable such as `$MY_BLOG_DB__URL`
# overrides one key of it.
ENV MY_BLOG_ADDR=0.0.0.0:3000 \
    MY_BLOG_ENV=production
EXPOSE 3000

# `/up` is the liveness probe of `observability-health`. Any HTTP answer
# passes, so a project without it passes with a 404: the process answers.
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=10s --retries=3 \
  CMD curl -sS -o /dev/null --max-time 2 http://127.0.0.1:3000/up || exit 1

# `serve` drains on SIGTERM for `[server] shutdown_grace_secs` (25 by
# default). Give `docker stop` more than that: `docker stop -t 30`.
STOPSIGNAL SIGTERM
CMD ["/app/my-blog", "serve"]
===== assets/css/_tailwind.css =====
/* The input of the CSS build. Never published.
 *
 * The leading underscore marks an input: a file that is bundled into another
 * and that `assets!` must not publish on its own. The build reads this file
 * and writes assets/css/site.css, which is the one that ships:
 *
 *     brocanth css          # once, minified
 *     brocanth css -W       # again on each change
 */

@import "tailwindcss";

/* Where the class names are.
 *
 * Tailwind resolves each path against this stylesheet and keeps its automatic
 * detection on as well. These are explicit for two reasons: `.rsx` is not an
 * extension Tailwind knows, and an explicit glob states the contract.
 */

/* The templates. A .rsx file is Rust with an HTML-shaped body. */
@source "../../src/pages/**/*.rsx";

/* Plain Rust holds class names too -- a helper that builds a class string,
 * or a handler choosing one. */
@source "../../src/**/*.rs";
===== assets/css/site.css =====
/* Written by `brocanth create` so the first build works.
   `brocanth css` replaces it with the real one. */
===== build.rs =====
//! Written by `brocanth`. Compiles `locales/*.toml` into `$OUT_DIR/i18n.rs`,
//! which `src/plugins/i18n/mod.rs` includes.
//!
//! The second argument is the default catalog: every other one is checked
//! against it, and a key it lacks is a build error. A key a translation
//! lacks is a warning, and that locale shows the default's text.

fn main() {
    brocanth_i18n::build::compile("locales", "en").unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
}
===== deploy/kubernetes.yaml =====
# A minimal deployment of this image. Change the image, the replica count
# and the secrets; keep the probes and the timings in step.
#
# The stop, in order:
#   1. SIGTERM. `/readyz` answers 503 at once, so the Service stops routing
#      here. The listener stays open for `drain_delay_secs` (5 below), while
#      the endpoint change reaches every node.
#   2. The listener closes. Open requests have what is left of
#      `shutdown_grace_secs` (25) to finish, then the plugins stop.
#   3. terminationGracePeriodSeconds (30) is the hard limit: SIGKILL. It is
#      above the 25, so a clean drain never meets it.
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: my-blog
spec:
  replicas: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: my-blog
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: my-blog
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 10001
      containers:
        - name: my-blog
          image: my-blog:latest
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 3000
          env:
            - name: MY_BLOG_SERVER__SHUTDOWN_GRACE_SECS
              value: "25"
            - name: MY_BLOG_OBSERVABILITY__DRAIN_DELAY_SECS
              value: "5"
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /up
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 10
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /readyz
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 1
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: my-blog
spec:
  selector:
    app: my-blog
  ports:
    - name: http
      port: 80
      targetPort: http
===== locales/en.toml =====
# The default catalog. Every key is here; `build.rs` names this file.
#
# A key is a function: `welcome` is `t::welcome(locale)`. `{name}` is an
# argument, and a table with `one` and `other` is a plural. A misspelt key
# or a missing argument is a compile error at the call. See the
# `brocanth-i18n` README.

welcome = "Welcome"
greeting = "Hello, {name}!"

[items]
"=0" = "No items"
one = "{count} item"
other = "{count} items"

# The consent banner of the `gtag` module. Its hook reads these keys in the
# request's locale. A key left out of every catalog falls back to the
# `[gtag.banner]` setting.
[gtag.banner]
text = "We would like to measure visits with Google Analytics. It sets cookies only if you accept."
accept = "Accept"
refuse = "Refuse"
manage = "Cookie settings"
policy = "Privacy policy"
